Request: reports/phase-06/review-request-2-liveness-rework.md (worktree HEAD 258905159; content pin eefe3c4e73012ad439010196d6690d546ca87c8e; pack commit ebb68bbf6d4e703f974456e2d4a51d630870f7ec; scan commit 5d470d7e4718585733b141fe28c0356697b3edd0; owner decisions A17 3d8ee0d11, A18 c071b6c66; A5/A6/A11-A18 govern)
Reading method: scan re-run and compared; every one of the 38 changed files read in full against its pre-pass version and the legacy source; each section classified and every runtime sentence checked against code with file:line or command evidence (nine parallel Sonnet sub-reviewers over disjoint file groups, each with the same written brief; the reviewer verified the headline findings and the data mechanics, the sub-reviewers' per-section notes are in the Note columns and below); source/retired/receipt rows judged from the section verdicts plus script checks. Reading was by section, not by sampling.

## Verdict

**Not accepted. The mechanics and the liveness scan are sound; the restored content is not yet true and current.** Of 38 changed files, 9 ok and 29 rework (0 hold). Rows: source 1046 ok / 241 rework / 11 hold of 1,298; retired 1090/76/8 of 1,174; receipts 1285/327/3 of 1,615.

**Rework rate over the changed rows (A17 item 5): 666 of 4087 judged rows = 16.3% (source 19.4%, retired 7.2%, receipts 20.4%). This is above the 3% stop rule.** Rework is concentrated in sections whose text was restored without being corrected (see below), not in carriage.

## 1. The liveness scan

- scan-coordination-liveness.mjs re-run at HEAD against source commit 7880fbc74 and liveness-classification-input.json: files section list byte-identical to the committed liveness-scan.json (68 files, 997 slices), liveInputs identical (569 tracked textual inputs in the nine required roots; 5 binary skipped).
- Roots covered: src/ (226 tracked files), packages/ (111), apps/ (66), bin/ (2), core/skills/ (29), domains/ (50), scripts/ (75), docs/specs/ (14) and AGENTS.md; excluded only secrets/node_modules/target/dist/build.
- Known live cases from the previous review: group-thinking-trigger-surface.md found in core/skills/fgos-panel/SKILL.md:37 (hit); ADR-006 found in src/runner/dispatch/assignment.mjs lines 3,9,17,27,33,130,... (hit); ADR-007 found in domains/coding/harness/enrich-and-validate-contract.mjs lines 2,7,16,... (hit); ADR-004 found in docs/specs/runner.md:1243 (hit); workflow-stage-operation.md: NOT found by the scan as a path/basename in src/setup/registrations.mjs (only the ratchet baseline cites the path). The file is still current by default, via the type-symbol path (TaskSpec hits) and the author's evidence; the real consumer is the function findWorkflowStageOperationProblems at src/setup/registrations.mjs:1037 (called :1781) and src/runner/operation-choice.mjs:733-757, referenced by concept not by file name.
- **False negatives:** (1) the scan matches path, basename, ADR id, camel-case type symbols (length >= 6) and file paths in the text; it cannot see concept-level consumers (workflow-stage-operation above; the live recover door). (2) 13 of 997 sections end with no-live-reference-found (semantic-cli-surface sections, spec.md scope/non-scope/gaps, implementation-alignment boundary note); none is retirement-eligible by the scan's own rule and none was moved. (3) For 22 historical files the only reference beyond the ratchet baseline is docs/specs/runner.md/reading-map.md citing the frozen docs/architect path, which the scan counts; correct. No file whose live reference is path/ADR-id based was missed. Net: no false negative changed a disposition in this pass because the pass protects by default; the scan is a protection device, not a proof of implementation, and the author says so.
- Protection works: wholeFileRetirementEligible is empty for all 68 files; no new whole-file move.

## 5. Per-file findings

### architecture/dispatch-control-plane.md

Verdict: rework. The file was restored from 34 to 344 lines and now carries Responsibility, Flow, Routing Identities, Contracts, Executor Kinds, Ownership, Governance and Source Inventory, and docs/specs/runner.md:1244 still cites it as the canonical home of DispatchRequest/PolicyPatch/DispatchPlan and the eight authorities. Large parts are true against code (DispatchPlan fields, EXECUTOR_KINDS, Gate B3, boundary tests, governance gate). But the restore re-imported engine-era or dead identifiers as current fact: the dead policy key minTier (L130, L195), the non-existent resolveExecutorIdForPurpose (L93), mergePolicyStack (L279), the absent cohort-planner.mjs exception (L300-303), OccupancyPort (L342), and a claim that capability overrides.rigorOverrides are folded into the synthesized policy (L234-236) although rigorOverrides is a flagged dead key and plan.mjs folds nothing of the kind. The Component-Outer Boundary Note section is an empty heading (L305-306) while L115 and L270 point to it, so the live outer-caller rule exists only in history. Separation Of Concerns lost its engine-independent 'dispatch does not care who proposed the Assignment' sentence. Verdict: rework.

### architecture/evidence-and-results.md

Verdict: rework. The file again carries Principle, Evidence Sources, Confidence Boundaries, Aggregation and Visibility Boundary, so the previous over-cut is fixed, and Evidence Sources, Principle and Visibility Boundary are supported by code and runner.md RUL40. Aggregation is an unimplemented normative rule that the status table frames as such (no majority/aggregation code exists in src/runner/execution or src/workflow). Confidence Boundaries now has a citation for every bullet, but three of the cited line ranges do not support the sentence: 'exit code zero cannot satisfy missing outputs' and 'missing/malformed evidence must not false-pass' cite the corrupt-record fail-closed block, and 'stale or cross-Run evidence must be rejected' cites runId-mismatch lines plus attribution lines that do not reject stale evidence (no stale handling exists). So the section is rework.

### architecture/visibility-and-herdr.md

Verdict: ok. Restored current contract for the herdr-as-visibility boundary: Purpose, five-line Invariant, Session Binding (visibility.json vs run.json.status, three-answer reconcile), Observer And Actor, Pane Retention, Allowed Uses, Forbidden Inferences and a Stability Direction that explicitly separates the implemented control-epoch work from the unimplemented writable-takeover/handle-store proposal. Every runtime-fact sentence I checked matches src/runner/dispatch/visibility-session.mjs, herdr-round.mjs, liveness.mjs and run-lock.mjs, and the obsolete pid-and-token actor-lease sentence was corrected (visibility-session.mjs:136-149 documents its removal). ### proposals/dispatch-control-plane-redesign.md

Verdict: rework. The rework restored the whole proposal as current (correct, since DispatchPlan, governance and herdr-spawn are live and runner.md plus two explanation docs cite it, with zero retired-engine terms). Sections 1-4 are now framed as dated context, sections 9, 11, 13, 15 are unimplemented design correctly labelled, and 7.1/7.2/14.2 match code. But the rework only added a one-paragraph banner: it did not split the implementation-status material. Sections 6.1-6.3, 7.3, 8.2, 10 (post-merge paragraph), 12.2, 14, 14.1, 14.3, 16 and 17.3 still state shipped behaviour in present tense that is false against code (herdr sentinel contract removed, asgn_/run_ ids exist, dispatch-confidence reader deleted, executor ids agy/pi gone, DispatchPlan shape differs). The header also lost the legacy pointer to architecture/dispatch-control-plane.md and now claims canonical status. No retired-engine (CoordinationSession/FlowDefinition) text is presented as current.

### architecture/group-thinking-trigger-surface.md

Verdict: rework. The restored file is the live preset map for fgos-panel. Its Surface Taxonomy table (L74-83), Clarification Rules, Core/Surface boundary and Entry Point Choice are true against core/skills/fgos-panel/SKILL.md, src/runner/execution/patterns/presets.mjs and core/workflows/*.yaml, so the liveness fix (restoring it as current) is right and the retired protocol-id column and coordination run/show row were correctly dropped. Remaining defects are narrow: L31-32 still describes the retired protocol-id pack gate; L72 inverts authority (says the table follows the skill although the skill and runner.md:2925 treat the table as canonical) and the "one canonical preset map" statement was lost; L89/L95 keep "-> advise" and "never fgos-code-panel"; Alternatives A, B and C state retired mechanisms (pack gate, protocol id, existing fgos-code-panel skill) as current. Both live consumers still cite the frozen docs/architect path rather than this file.

### proposals/team-communication-protocol-v1.md

Verdict: rework. The file was correctly split: sections 1-11 and 13 (roles, modes, step-operation selection, assignment prompt, agent-result schema, confidence ladder, stage protocols) are engine-independent and largely match code (domains/coding/registry.yaml, feature.yaml, assignment.mjs, agent-result-claim-contract.mjs, run-result.mjs); section 12 (mission-lite) was reduced to a historical pointer. Residual defects: section 6 calls agent-report.md optional and section 7 lets evidenceRefs satisfy a read-only done claim, both contradicted by run-result.mjs:1274-1280 and assignment.mjs:866-905; section 2 states a Mission boundary with no runtime; section 1 still advertises the removed prototype; section 5 misdescribes the operationsForStep signature history; section 10.3 uses stale future tense. Most of these are narrow edits, but 6 and 7 are shipped-behaviour inaccuracies.

### vision.md

Verdict: rework. vision.md is the restored Agent Coordination Vision with V-001, V-002, V-004, V-005, V-007, V-008, V-010, V-011, V-012 and the responsibility sections kept as normative direction, and most of it holds against code (execution-contract.mjs, assignment.mjs, run-result.mjs, scripts/dispatch-decide-hook.mjs). The rework removed engine text by deleting list items and whole decisions but left headings and lead-in sentences behind: "Rejected Interpretations" (lines 280-282) and "Open Design Questions" (lines 310-315) now announce lists that no longer exist. The removed material was not all retired-engine: Rejected Interpretations 4, 5, 7, 8 (skill prose may not call executors, Work not required, coding rules not in core, neutrality is not no-policy) and V-006 (domain harness enriches/rejects agent proposals, live as the ADR-007 seam in assignment.mjs) are engine-independent. V-004 line 181 and Problem 3 (lines 114-117) are stale against code (inline contract schema is decided and implemented; mission-lite.mjs no longer exists). "Reading Down" still routes readers to frozen docs/architect copies and to Step 07/08 (retired engine) as the place the open design is "resolved", and the "required second read" ledger link points at a non-authority history snapshot.

### vocabulary/canonical-concepts.md

Verdict: rework. canonical-concepts.md is now a live glossary of Work, Workflow, Stage, Stage Operation, TaskSpec, Skill, Role, Assignment, Capability, DispatchPlan, Dispatcher, Executor, Run, RunResult, Evidence, Artifact, Herdr plus five clearly labelled cognitive/proposed terms; the retired engine terms (FlowDefinition, Coordination Protocol, CoordinationSession, SessionActor, AdhocTask, Phase, Mission) are correctly gone to history. Most entries check out against code, and the earlier eight-domain failure list is no longer in this file (the failure taxonomy is not restated; FAILURE_FAMILIES is run-result.mjs:20). Four live defects remain: Capability still names `capabilities.<name>.overrides`, a key the config validator now rejects; TaskSpec says the inline contract representation is "under design" though it is implemented; Work says Work "may reference coordination sessions", a retired entity; and the `### Job` heading is left empty although Job is a live reserved term (ADR-004, runner.md:1243). Persona drops the live `policy.preferPersona` path and refers to SessionActor which no longer has an entry.

"Coordination Rings" names Orchestrator/Launcher/Router with no referent in code or any current document.

### playbooks/architecture-advisory-evaluation-rubric.md

Verdict: rework. The file is a candidate cognitive evaluation rubric for advisory sessions, with a verified Current Boundary (same text as the templates file) and two dimensions (9, 10) rewritten against the registered Workflow with correct line citations. Those rewrites, the framing sections, dimension 12, the overall verdict and the non-measure list are sound. But dimensions 1-8 and 11 still tell an evaluator to look in intake.md, interpretation.md, scout-report.md, decision-request.md, proposals/, critiques/, synthesis.md, dispositions.md, explanation.md and 'Phase 5/8', none of which the registered six-step Workflow produces, and the file has no mapping or manual-harness statement (the templates file at least has L27). Dimension 11 additionally assumes falsification criteria and run-record timestamps that the registered shaping tasks never produce. Two of four failed-review defects are fixed (dimensions 9, 10); the Phase/filename defect is not. No live content is missing; the only live consumer is SKILL.md:137-140.

About 150 of 216 units (posture, notice, heuristics, anti-pattern and example prose for the nine roles) are sound as cognitive doctrine. The generic disclaimer at line 72 does not reach these sections. One live paragraph (diversity is a hedge, SKILL.md:121-122) remains only in history.

### playbooks/README.md

Verdict: rework. Collection index for the playbooks directory, restored current. Bullets 1-2 (operating harness, master prompt) and the code listing of production authoring sources are accurate against the tree. Bullets 3-4 describe retired-engine handoffs (Step 07 CoordinationSession discussion, MVP6+ dogfood via `fgos coordination`) in present tense although the CLI has no coordination verb; the Bootstrap Role and End-State Lifecycle text still treat a CoordinationSession/AdhocTask runtime as a pending future. The directory-wide Runtime Boundary claim that no Skill may reference the playbooks is contradicted by the registered architecture-advisory skill, and the index omits the four advisory companion files the skill cites.

Role model, trace/current-cell/cell-trace contracts, resume rules, master-prompt hand-off and token budget (sections 2, 4, 6-8, 10-11) are internally consistent with the master prompt and with the verification/<track>/ convention in use. ### playbooks/prompts/master-coordinator.md

Verdict: rework. One-entry manual coordinator prompt, restored current per the owner decision. The role set, state machine A-J, status vocabulary, parallelism policy, context budget and the self-hosted hook hazard are accurate against the tree (scripts/dispatch-decide-hook.mjs imports src/runner/dispatch.mjs; the quoted smoke command prints a mechanism field). It carries real stale or false sentences: the Retirement section names `.agents/skills/fgos-code-change`, a skill that no longer exists (replaced by fgos-run); the persistent-state, playbook and example paths use the frozen docs/architect tree; the example paste names a plan directory that moved to archive/plans and a superseded test command; and the mechanism priority section states a Step-07-era ordering as universal and names an adapter ("rpc/app-server") that is not registered.

- fixed: Owner question (hold) on class (i) vs live reference role (review-2whole-files.md:28,65) (file present as current; area README.md:64 lists it; core hook hazard text verified (scripts/dispatch-decide-hook.mjs:25))
- NOT fixed: Retirement path points to .agents/skills/fgos-code-change which is absent, so the stated retirement did not happen (review-2whole-files.md:65) (master-coordinator.md:586-588 unchanged; git ls-files \| grep fgos-code-change -> none; CHANGELOG.md:154 (replaced by core/skills/fgos-run/SKILL.md); test/runner/dead-vocabulary-guard.test.mjs:415 lists fgos-code-change as a dead symbol)
